### [1] [Early Detection of Alzheimer's Disease Using Explainable Machine Learning on Clinical Biomarkers: A Multi-Class Classification Study Using the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03995)
*Afshan Hashmi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用XGBoost分类器，基于ADNI数据集的8项临床指标（MMSE、CDR Global、CDR-SB、MoCA、FAQ、年龄、性别、教育程度），结合SMOTE处理类别不平衡和Optuna超参数优化，实现了正常认知、轻度认知障碍和阿尔茨海默病的三分类检测。五折交叉验证宏平均AUC为0.983，测试集上AUC为0.982，准确率0.943。SHAP分析揭示了临床可解释的特征重要性模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球超过5500万AD患者，临床亟需一种能从常规临床评估中准确、可解释地检测NC、MCI和AD的方法。

Method: 基于ADNI数据集的8项临床特征，使用XGBoost分类器进行三分类检测。采用Optuna进行50次超参数优化，SMOTE处理类别不平衡。使用宏AUC-ROC（含1000次bootstrap 95%置信区间）、宏F1、平衡准确率和Cohen's kappa评估性能。SHAP值提供特征级可解释性。

Result: 五折交叉验证：宏平均AUC 0.983（SD 0.007）、准确率0.944（SD 0.006）、宏F1 0.929（SD 0.008）。测试集：宏AUC 0.982（95% CI: 0.965--0.995）、准确率0.943、平衡准确率0.932、宏F1 0.927、Cohen's kappa 0.909。SHAP分析显示CDR Global是NC和MCI的主要预测因子，CDR-SB和MMSE共同驱动AD分类。

Conclusion: 基于常规临床评估的可解释机器学习模型实现了近乎完美的AD三分类检测。SHAP分析揭示了临床合理且类别特定的特征重要性模式，支持模型的临床有效性。未来将融合语音生物标志物进行多模态检测。

Abstract: Background: Alzheimer's disease (AD) affects over 55 million people worldwide. Accurate, interpretable detection of normal cognition (NC), mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and AD from routine clinical assessments remains a critical unmet need. Methods: An XGBoost classifier was developed for three-class detection using eight clinical features from the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI): MMSE, CDR Global, CDR Sum of Boxes (CDR-SB), MoCA, FAQ, age, sex, and education. Hyperparameters were optimised using Optuna (50 trials); class imbalance was addressed with SMOTE. Performance was evaluated by macro AUC-ROC with 1,000-iteration bootstrap 95% confidence intervals, macro F1, balanced accuracy, and Cohen's kappa. SHAP values provided feature-level explainability. Results: The dataset comprised 1,641 baseline subjects (608 NC, 767 MCI, 266 AD). On five-fold cross-validation, mean macro AUC was 0.983 (SD 0.007), accuracy 0.944 (SD 0.006), and macro F1 0.929 (SD 0.008). On the held-out test set (n = 247), macro AUC was 0.982 (95% CI: 0.965--0.995), accuracy 0.943, balanced accuracy 0.932, macro F1 0.927, and Cohen's kappa 0.909. SHAP analysis identified CDR Global as the dominant predictor for NC and MCI, while CDR-SB and MMSE together drove AD classification. Conclusion: An explainable machine learning model trained on routine clinical assessments achieves near-perfect three-class Alzheimer's detection. SHAP analysis reveals clinically plausible, class-specific feature importance patterns supporting clinical validity. Future work will extend this framework with speech biomarkers for multimodal detection.

</details>


### [2] [Novel Aspects of IEEE SA P3109 Arithmetic Formats for Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04028)
*Andrew Fitzgibbon,Christoph M. Wintersteiger,Jeffrey Sarnoff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IEEE P3109草案标准定义了一组参数化的二进制浮点格式和运算，专为机器学习优化，支持宽度、精度、符号性和无穷大的参数化，采用封闭扩展实数集定义运算，包含多种舍入和饱和模式（包括随机舍入），无异常运算，通过返回值（如NaN）传递异常，支持块级缩放运算，并通过κ-近似提供尺度不变的近似实现度量，所有标准函数定义均通过形式化规范进行机械验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为机器学习领域提供高效、一致的少位数浮点表示标准，解决传统浮点格式在ML场景中位宽不灵活、异常处理开销大、近似实现缺乏统一度量等问题。

Method: 采用参数化浮点格式族（宽度、精度、符号性、无穷性可调），将浮点值解码到封闭扩展实数集（含±∞和NaN）以分离实数运算与特殊值处理，定义无异常运算体系（通过返回值传递异常），引入随机舍入、块级共享缩放因子运算，以及κ-近似（尺度不变的ulp类比）用于描述近似实现，通过形式化规范进行机械验证。

Result: 定义了参数化浮点格式族及其完整运算集，支持多种舍入/饱和模式，实现无异常吞吐加速，块级缩放运算统一化，以及κ-近似度量标准。标准函数定义和性质经形式化规范机械验证和生成。

Conclusion: 该标准为机器学习场景提供了一套灵活、高效、形式化验证的浮点运算体系，通过参数化设计适应不同精度需求，通过无异常和近似度量机制优化吞吐性能，具有良好的可扩展性和实现灵活性。

Abstract: The IEEE P3109 draft standard defines a parameterized family of binary floating-point formats and associated operations, with a focus on facilitating machine learning. These formats allow efficient and consistent representation of values in a small number of bits. The defined formats are parameterized over width and precision in bits, signedness, and the presence of infinities. Operations are defined by decoding floating-point values to the set of closed extended reals: the reals augmented with positive and negative infinity and NaN (Not a Number). Explicit treatment of NaN and infinite operands ensures that only real arithmetic is invoked in operation definitions. Extensive rounding and saturation modes are defined; stochastic rounding is included. Operations are exception-free, accelerating throughput, with exceptional situations communicated through return values, e.g., NaN. Operations on blocks of values sharing a common scale factor are defined in terms of the underlying operations in a uniform manner. System vendors may describe approximate implementations via a novel scale-invariant measure, akin to units in the last place, called kappa-approximation. Standard function definitions and various other properties are mechanically verified and generated using formal specifications.

</details>


### [3] [Position: Deployed Reinforcement Learning should be Continual](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04029)
*Parnian Behdin,Kevin Roice,Golnaz Mesbahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了部署后无法达到最优但能接收评估奖励信号的智能体本质上是一个持续强化学习问题，识别了部署后导致非平稳性的四个来源，分析了真实世界中持续RL的成功案例，并提出了摆脱当前"训练-修复"范式的优势与措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数强化学习系统采用"训练-修复"范式，即训练好的智能体在与环境交互时不再学习，直到性能下降才重新训练。作者认为这种范式存在根本性问题，需要转向持续学习范式。

Method: 本文是一篇立场论文，通过理论论证识别了部署后四个非平稳性来源（概念漂移、环境变化、任务迁移、分布偏移等），并分析了真实世界中持续RL的成功案例。

Result: 明确了部署后智能体必须持续学习的四个非平稳性来源，以及最佳部署智能体从不停止适应的原因。

Conclusion: 倡导RL社区从当前主流的"训练-修复"范式转向持续RL范式，并提供了相应的优势分析和实施措施。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has received increasing attention and adoption in real-world use cases. Most of these systems follow a train-then-fix paradigm, where trained agents do not learn while interacting with the world until performance degrades and retraining becomes necessary. In this position paper, we argue that deploying an agent that is incapable of optimality, but receives an evaluative reward signal, is inherently a continual RL problem. We identify four sources of non-stationarity after deployment that necessitate never-ending learning, and highlight why the best deployed agents never stop adapting. We analyze successful examples of continual RL in the real world, and present the community with the advantages and measures to move away from the current train-then-fix paradigm.

</details>


### [4] [Pseudospectral Bounds for Transient Amplification in Coupled Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04031)
*Ahanaf Hasan Ariq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了耦合梯度下降中块三角雅可比矩阵的非正态性导致的瞬态放大现象，建立了尖锐的伪谱理论，给出了Kreiss常数的上下界，并推导了有限步迭代复杂度界限，揭示了谱半径分析无法捕捉的高维学习动力学非渐近、实例依赖的标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合梯度下降（如双层优化、两时间尺度随机逼近、对抗训练）中，当耦合雅可比矩阵为块三角形式时，即使谱半径保证渐近稳定性，非正态性可能导致收敛前的瞬态放大任意大，现有谱半径分析无法刻画这一现象。

Method: 建立了块三角雅可比矩阵的尖锐伪谱理论：证明了当对角块对称且谱半径≤γ<1时，Kreiss常数K(J) ≤ 2/(1-γ) + ‖C‖/(4(1-γ))，并给出极小极大下界；刻画了临界耦合阈值；通过Neumann级数微扰框架扩展至近自指系统。

Result: 得到随机耦合下降的有限步迭代复杂度界O(K(J)² log(1/δ))；揭示了谱半径分析无法捕捉的非渐近、实例依赖的高维学习动力学标度律；在线性二次问题、IQC比较和神经网络训练中验证了理论。

Conclusion: 本文的伪谱理论为理解耦合优化中的瞬态行为提供了严格框架，证明了非正态性是导致耦合梯度下降中瞬态放大的关键机制，所建立的迭代复杂度界限具有实用价值。

Abstract: Coupled gradient descent--where the update of one parameter block depends on another--underlies bilevel optimization, two-time-scale stochastic approximation, and adversarial training. When the coupled Jacobian is block-triangular, asymptotic stability is governed by the spectral radii of the diagonal blocks, yet transient amplification before convergence can be arbitrarily large due to non-normality. We develop a sharp pseudospectral theory for such block-triangular Jacobians, proving that the Kreiss constant satisfies $K(J) \leq 2/(1-γ) + \|C\|/(4(1-γ))$ when the diagonal blocks are symmetric with spectral radii at most $γ< 1$, and we establish matching minimax lower bounds. We characterize the critical coupling threshold for spectral instability and extend the analysis to nearly self-referential systems via a Neumann-series perturbation framework. As a consequence, we obtain a finite-horizon iteration-complexity bound of $O(K(J)^2 \log(1/δ))$ for stochastic coupled descent. Framed as scaling laws for non-stationary two-time-scale optimization, our results expose a non-asymptotic, instance-dependent regime of high-dimensional learning dynamics that is invisible to spectral-radius analysis. Experiments on linear-quadratic problems, IQC-based comparisons, and neural-network training confirm the theory.

</details>


### [5] [Do Transformers Need Three Projections? Systematic Study of QKV Variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04032)
*Ali Kayyam,Anusha Madan Gopal,M Anthony Lewis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Transformer中QKV投影共享的三种变体，发现Q-K=V共享（共享Key-Value投影）在保持性能的同时可显著减少KV缓存，与GQA/MQA结合后缓存减少可达87.5%-96.9%，特别适合边缘设备部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Transformer标准采用QKV三种独立投影，但每种投影的个体贡献以及省略某些投影的影响尚不清楚。作者希望系统性地探究投影共享对模型性能和效率的影响。

Method: 研究了三种投影共享约束：a) Q-K=V（共享Key-Value），b) Q=K-V（共享Query-Key），c) Q=K=V（单投影）。针对对称注意力问题引入了2D位置编码实现非对称注意力。在合成任务、视觉任务和语言模型（300M和1.2B参数，10B tokens）上进行了广泛实验。

Result: Q-K=V共享性能与标准QKV相当甚至更优；在语言建模中仅造成3.1%困惑度下降但实现50% KV缓存减少；与GQA-4结合实现87.5%缓存减少，与MQA结合实现96.9%缓存减少。

Conclusion: Q-K=V能保持质量是因为Key和Value可以占据相似的表示空间且注意力在低秩机制下运行，而Q=K-V破坏了注意力的方向性。投影共享是注意力机制中一种被低估的权重绑定形式，对边缘部署具有直接可量化的推理内存优势。

Abstract: Transformers have become the standard solution for various AI tasks, with the query, key, and value (QKV) attention formulation playing a central role. However, the individual contribution of these three projections and the impact of omitting some remain poorly understood. We systematically evaluate three projection sharing constraints: a) Q-K=V (shared key-value), b) Q=K-V (shared query-key), and c) Q=K=V (single projection). The last two variants produce symmetric attention maps; to address this, we also explore asymmetric attention via 2D positional encodings. Through experiments spanning synthetic tasks, vision (MNIST, CIFAR, TinyImageNet, anomaly), and language modeling (300M and 1.2B parameter models on 10B tokens), we discovered that our transformers perform on par or occasionally better than the QKV transformer. In language modeling, Q-K=V projection sharing achieves 50% KV cache reduction with only 3.1% perplexity degradation. Crucially, projection sharing is complementary to head sharing (GQA/MQA): combining Q-K=V with GQA-4 yields 87.5% cache reduction, while Q-K=V + MQA achieves 96.9%, enabling practical on-device inference. We show that Q-K=V preserves quality because keys and values can occupy similar representational spaces and attention operates in a low-rank regime, whereas Q=K-V breaks attention directionality. Our results systematically characterize projection sharing as an underexplored instance of weight tying in attention, with direct, quantifiable inference memory benefits, particularly valuable for edge deployment. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/anushamadan02/Do-Transformers-Need-3-Projections

</details>


### [6] [Inverse Critical Experiment Design via Gradient Optimization and a Multigroup Attention-Based Neural Network Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04033)
*Will Savage,Logan Burnett,Dean Price*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度神经网络代理模型和非参数梯度优化的临界实验逆设计方法，通过最大化中子学相似性系数$c_k$来生成实验几何结构，应用于HALEU燃料运输容器的验证，最高达到0.97757的$c_k$值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进核反应堆设计和燃料概念的验证需要与目标技术具有高中子学相似性的临界实验，而现有实验覆盖不足。传统方法难以高效设计出满足$c_k\geq0.9$要求的实验几何结构。

Method: 使用U-Net卷积编码器-解码器结合多组注意力池化层构建深度神经网络代理模型，在OpenMC计算的网格化临界实验几何灵敏度向量上训练；利用代理模型的可微性，通过非参数梯度优化直接改变几何网格中每个位置的材料分配来最大化$c_k$。

Result: 针对TN-Americas TN-LC运输容器（HALEU燃料）三种感兴趣构型，优化生成的实验几何分别达到0.97757、0.81324和0.93276的$c_k$值，其中两种构型超过0.9的阈值。

Conclusion: 深度学习代理模型结合梯度优化能够有效加速临界实验的逆设计过程，为先进核技术的验证提供有力工具。

Abstract: The validation of advanced nuclear reactor designs and fuel concepts requires critical experiments with high neutronic similarity to the target technology. Neutronic similarity is quantified by the correlation coefficient $c_k$, which captures the shared bias in $k_\text{eff}$ induced by uncertainties in nuclear data. Generally, a $c_k\geq0.9$ is needed for an experiment to be sufficiently similar to a target technology. This work presents a methodology for the inverse design of critical experiments. Deep neural network surrogate modeling and nonparametric gradient optimization are used to generate experiment geometries that maximize $c_k$.
  A deep neural network is trained on OpenMC-calculated sensitivity vectors for grid-based critical experiment geometries. The model architecture combines a U-Net convolutional encoder-decoder with a novel multigroup attention pooling layer, introduced to capture the differing spatial dependencies of sensitivities. Multigroup attention pooling is shown to achieve better performance than traditional pooling, as well as interpretable internal behavior. The differentiability of the surrogate enables gradient-based optimization of the full combinatorial design space, allowing $c_k$ to be maximized by directly changing the material assignment of each position in the geometry grid.
  The method is applied to the validation of the TN-Americas TN-LC transportation cask with HALEU fuel, for which existing critical experiment coverage is limited. The optimization procedure is shown to produce experiment geometries achieving $c_k$ scores of 0.97757, 0.81324, and 0.93276 for three configurations of interest. This approach demonstrates the potential of deep learning and gradient optimization to accelerate the development of advanced nuclear technology.

</details>


### [7] [Self-Distilled Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04036)
*Yifeng Liu,Shiyuan Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SDPG是一个结合组相对验证器优势、归一化标准差、精确全词汇在线策略自蒸馏和参考策略KL正则化的自蒸馏策略梯度框架，在多轮RLVR对比实验中表现出更好的稳定性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略自蒸馏能够为稀疏奖励强化学习提供密集监督信号，但现有方法在稳定性和性能上仍有提升空间。

Method: 提出SDPG框架：1) 组相对验证器优势（group-relative verifier advantages）；2) 归一化标准差；3) 精确全词汇在线策略自蒸馏（作为学生-教师反向KL散度损失实现）；4) 参考策略KL正则化。

Result: SDPG在稳定性与性能上均优于RLVR和自蒸馏基线方法。

Conclusion: 自蒸馏可以实例化为全词汇学生-教师反向KL散度损失，结合多种正则化手段能有效提升强化学习训练效果。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation, where a language model conditions on privileged context to supervise its own generations, is a promising source of dense supervision for sparse-reward reinforcement learning. Actually, it can be instantiated as an auxiliary full-vocabulary student-to-teacher reverse Kullback-Leibler divergence loss. We therefore propose SDPG, a self-distilled policy-gradient framework that combines group-relative verifier advantages with normalized standard deviation, exact full-vocabulary on-policy self-distillation, as well as reference-policy KL regularization. Empirically, SDPG improves stability and performance over RLVR and self-distillation baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/lauyikfung/SDPG.

</details>


### [8] [EvalStop: Using World Feedback to Detect and Correct Reward Overoptimization in Multi-Tenant RLHF Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04145)
*Guilin Zhang,Chuanyi Sun,Shahryar Sarkani,John M. Fossaceca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了EvalStop，一种基于评估分数连续下降来终止过优化RLHF任务的调度原语，在RLHF密集型工作负载上实现98%精度/99%召回率，同时JCT降低9%、浪费计算减少22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云LLM微调平台的调度器忽略了RLHF中奖励过优化问题——代理奖励模型在持续优化压力下会偏离真实世界反馈。非先知调度器完全忽略质量信号，SLAQ式调度器使用单调下降的训练损失（易被hack），而经典per-job早停需要人工监控且不释放共享GPU。

Method: 提出EvalStop组合式调度原语：当任务连续k次评估分数下降时终止作业、释放GPU、保存最佳检查点，并可委托给任意基础调度器。将调度器级早停建模为检测问题，在离散事件模拟器上评估，混合了奖励hacking和结构健康运行的RLHF工作负载。

Result: 在80% RLHF、64 GPU的负载下：精度98%、召回99%、假阳性率1.5%；相比SRTF-Est，JCT改善9%、浪费计算减少22%（p<0.05）。固定进度和损失停滞的竞争者要么在健康RLHF上产生65% FPR，要么漏掉超半数的hacking案例。各基础调度器上JCT提升9-25%；评估噪声标准差≤0.05时精度≥91%，hacking比例20-80%时精度≥89%。

Conclusion: EvalStop作为调度级早停原语能有效检测和终止奖励过优化的RLHF任务，在检测质量和调度性能上均优于现有方法，且具有良好的组合性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cloud LLM fine-tuning platforms increasingly serve RLHF workloads, where a learned reward model is optimized as a proxy for human quality. As Gao et al. (2023) showed, this proxy diverges from world feedback (downstream eval metrics) under sustained optimization pressure, a phenomenon known as reward overoptimization. Existing platform schedulers ignore this divergence: non-clairvoyant schedulers optimize JCT without any quality signal, SLAQ-style quality-aware schedulers use training loss (a weaker proxy that drops monotonically through hacking), and classical per-job early stopping requires human monitoring and does not free shared GPUs. We propose EvalStop, a composable scheduling primitive that terminates jobs on k consecutive eval-score declines, releases GPUs, preserves the best checkpoint, and delegates to any base scheduler. We frame scheduler-level early stopping as a detection problem and evaluate it in a discrete-event simulator whose RLHF workload mixes reward-hacking and structurally healthy runs, with ground-truth labels hidden from schedulers. On RLHF-heavy workloads (80% RLHF, 64 GPUs), EvalStop achieves precision 98% / recall 99% / FPR 1.5% while improving JCT by 9% and cutting wasted compute by 22% over SRTF-Est (p<0.05). Trivial fixed-progress and loss-plateau competitors either incur 65% FPR on healthy RLHF or miss over half of true hacking cases. Gains compose across every base scheduler tested (9-25% JCT) and detection quality stays stable under eval noise (precision at least 91% at noise std <= 0.05) and hacking base rate (precision at least 89% across 20-80% hacking fractions).

</details>


### [9] [Bayes-Sufficient Representations in Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04045)
*Vasileios Sevetlidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文定义了贝叶斯充分表示的概念，指出对于一个固定的监督决策问题，表示是否充分取决于损失函数和联合分布。在贝叶斯动作几乎必然唯一的情况下，核心对象是贝叶斯商（Bayes quotient），它识别需要相同贝叶斯最优动作的输入。表示充分当且仅当它精炼了这个商，贝叶斯最小表示则与之信息等价。该框架与属性提取消（property elicitation）自然连接，并通过实验展示了充分性、最小性和保留非必要信息之间的区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示学习通常说"保留输入中与预测相关的信息"，但"相关"的含义在固定监督决策问题下并不明确。本文旨在严格定义在给定联合分布和损失函数下，什么构成了一个充分（sufficient）的表示，并找出为实现贝叶斯最优预测所需的最少信息。

Method: 从决策论出发，将表示定义为编码函数，称其为贝叶斯充分的（Bayes-sufficient），如果存在某个预测头可以用该表示实现贝叶斯最优决策规则。引入贝叶斯商（Bayes quotient）的概念——即输入空间上诱导出相同贝叶斯最优动作的等价关系。表示充分当且仅当它精炼（refines）这个商；表示贝叶斯最小（Bayes-minimal）当且仅当它与商信息等价。通过与属性提取消（property elicitation）的联系，分析了不同损失（零一损失、平方损失、Brier损失、对数损失等）对应的最小充分表示。

Result: 证明了不同损失函数对应不同的贝叶斯商：零一损失对应贝叶斯类（Bayes class），平方损失对应条件均值，Brier损失对应二元预测中的条件概率，对数损失或严格恰当评分规则对应完整的预测分布。通过控制实验、神经瓶颈实验和iNaturalist数据实验，展示了充分性、最小性以及保留非必要信息之间的区别。

Conclusion: 对于固定的监督问题，分布和损失决定了贝叶斯动作，贝叶斯动作决定了商，而商决定了贝叶斯最优预测所需的最小信息。表示学习应关注目标损失函数所对应的贝叶斯商，而非无条件地保留输入中的所有信息。

Abstract: Representation learning is often described as preserving the information in an input that is relevant for prediction. This work asks what relevance means for a fixed supervised decision problem. A representation is defined to be Bayes-sufficient for a joint distribution and loss if some prediction head can use it to implement a Bayes-optimal action rule. This makes the target information loss-dependent. In the almost-surely unique Bayes-action case, the relevant object is a Bayes quotient, which identifies inputs that require the same Bayes-optimal action. A representation is sufficient when it refines this quotient, and Bayes-minimal when it is informationally equivalent to it. The framework connects naturally to property elicitation: zero-one loss requires the Bayes class, squared loss the conditional mean, Brier loss the conditional probability in binary prediction, and log loss or strictly proper scoring rules the predictive distribution. Controlled finite experiments, learned neural bottleneck experiments, and a real-data iNaturalist taxonomic refinement experiment illustrate the distinction between sufficiency, minimality, and retained non-required information. For a fixed supervised problem, the distribution and the loss determine the Bayes action, the Bayes action determines the quotient, and the quotient determines the minimal information required for Bayes-optimal prediction.

</details>


### [10] [Unlocking Feature Learning in Gated Delta Networks at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04048)
*Yifeng Liu,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过严格的坐标尺度分析，推导了Gated Delta Network在最大更新参数化（μP）框架下的缩放规则，使得学习率能够跨模型宽度稳定迁移。实验验证了该方法在AdamW和SGD优化器下的有效性，而标准参数化无法实现迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练和扩展大型语言模型需要巨大的计算资源，因此需要高效的次二次架构和 principled 的超参数调优方法。虽然μP已实现标准Transformer的零样本超参数迁移，但其扩展到线性模型（尤其是具有结构化状态转移和复杂架构的模型）尚未被探索。

Method: 通过严格的前向传播、门控机制和循环状态动力学中的坐标尺度估计传播，推导了Gated Delta Network的缩放规则。

Result: 语言模型预训练实验证实，所提出的配置在AdamW和SGD下均能实现跨模型宽度的稳定学习率迁移，而标准参数化则无法迁移。

Conclusion: 验证了分析的正确性和实际效用，为Gated Delta Network等线性模型的高效超参数迁移提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Training and scaling Large Language Models demand enormous computational resources, motivating both efficient sub-quadratic architectures and principled hyperparameter tuning methods. While the Maximal Update Parametrization ($μ$P) has enabled zero-shot hyperparameter transfer for standard Transformers, its extension to linear models, particularly those with structured state transitions and complicated architectures, remains largely unexplored. By rigorously propagating coordinate-size estimates through the forward pass, gating mechanisms, and recurrent state dynamics, we derive the scaling rules for Gated Delta Network. Experiments on language-model pre-training confirm that our configurations enable stable learning-rate transfer across model widths under both AdamW and SGD, whereas standard parametrization fails to transfer, validating the correctness and practical utility of our analysis.

</details>


### [11] [LiftQuant: Continuous Bit-Width LLM via Dimensional Lifting and Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04050)
*Liulu He,XuanAng Liu,Juntao Liu,Taolue Feng,Ting Lu,Chunsheng Gan,Zhiyv Peng,Yuan Du,Huanrui Yang,Yijiang Liu,Li Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiftQuant提出了一种"提升-投影"机制，通过在高维空间中投影1比特晶格实现连续比特宽度控制（如2.4比特），使70B大模型可精确适配24GB GPU，性能显著优于传统2比特模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化方法受限于整数比特宽度（如2、3比特），存在"部署鸿沟"——大语言模型无法针对特定内存预算进行最优适配，因此需要一种能实现连续比特宽度控制的量化框架。

Method: 提出"提升-投影"机制：在高维"提升"空间中通过1比特晶格投影来近似低维权重向量；有效比特宽度由提升维度与原始维度的比值决定，通过灵活调整维度实现准连续比特宽度调节；该投影产生结构化非均匀码本，具备向量量化的表达能力，但解码路径仅依赖线性变换和1比特均匀量化器，保持硬件友好性。

Result: LiftQuant可将70B大模型压缩至2.4比特，精确适配24GB GPU，性能显著超越在同一设备上部署的现有最优2比特模型。

Conclusion: LiftQuant通过连续比特宽度控制弥合了部署鸿沟，实现了真正的Pareto最优部署，兼顾了量化灵活性与硬件友好性。

Abstract: Existing quantization methods are fundamentally limited by rigid, integer-based bit-widths (e.g., 2, 3-bit), resulting in a ``deployment gap" where Large Language Models cannot be optimally fitted to specific memory budgets. To bridge this gap, we introduce LiftQuant, a novel framework that enables continuous bit-width control for true Pareto-optimal deployment. The core innovation is a ``lift-then-project" mechanism which approximates low-dimensional weight vectors by projecting a simple 1-bit lattice from a higher-dimensional ``lifted" space. Crucially, the effective bit-width is determined simply by the ratio of the lifted dimension to the original dimension, which allows the bit-width to be tuned quasi-continuous as the dimension is a flexible structural parameter. This projection generates a structured yet non-uniform codebook, capturing the expressive power of Vector Quantization (VQ). While beneficial over VQ, LiftQuant's decoding path relies solely on linear transformations and 1-bit uniform quantizers, retaining hardware-friendly nature. This flexibility is transformative: LiftQuant enables a 70B LLM to be compressed to 2.4 bits to precisely fit a 24GB GPU, where its performance significantly surpasses state-of-the-art 2-bit models fitted on the same device. Our code and ckpt is available at https://github.com/Heliulu/LiftQuant.

</details>


### [12] [RUBAS: Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning for Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04051)
*Xian Qi Loye,Qinglin Su,Zhexin Zhang,Shiyao Cui,Qi Zhu,Fei Mi,Hongning Wang,Minlie Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RUBAS是一个基于rubric的强化学习框架，通过将智能体行为分解为工具使用安全、参数安全、响应安全和有用性四个维度，提供细粒度可解释奖励，在保障安全性的同时维持任务完成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM演变为工具增强型智能体后，面临与传统文本生成不同的安全挑战（真实世界执行风险）。现有对齐方法依赖粗粒度的拒绝信号或静态监督，难以在多样化的智能体风险中平衡安全性与有用的工具执行。

Method: 提出RUBAS框架，将智能体行为分解为四个维度（工具使用安全、参数安全、响应安全、有用性），基于结构化rubric在完整智能体轨迹上提供细粒度、可解释的奖励信号，通过强化学习优化安全工具使用同时保持任务完成度。

Result: 在多个智能体安全基准和模型上的实验表明，RUBAS相比标准对齐基线提升了安全性，减少了工具相关的幻觉，并保持了有竞争力的实用性。

Conclusion: 多维度rubric奖励能够为LLM智能体在安全关键的工具使用场景中提供有效的训练信号。

Abstract: The evolution of LLMs into tool-enabled agents creates a new class of safety challenges associated with real-world execution rather than simple text generation. Existing alignment methods often rely on coarse refusal signals or static supervision, making it difficult to balance safety with useful tool execution across diverse agentic risks. We introduce RUBAS, a rubric-based reinforcement learning framework for agent safety. RUBAS decomposes agent behavior into four dimensions: tool-use safety, argument safety, response safety, and helpfulness. These structured rubrics provide fine-grained and interpretable rewards over complete agent trajectories, enabling reinforcement learning to optimize safe tool use while preserving task completion. Extensive experiments across multiple agent safety benchmarks and models show that RUBAS improves safety over standard alignment baselines, reduces tool-grounded hallucinations, and maintains competitive utility. Our results suggest that multi-dimensional rubric rewards provide an effective training signal for aligning LLM agents in safety-critical tool-use settings.

</details>


### [13] [A Goal-Set Characterization of Task Composition in the Boolean Task Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04053)
*Eduardo Terrés-Caballero,Herke van Hoof*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了布尔任务代数（BTA）中一个结构性坍缩现象：在确定性MDP中，最优扩展Q值函数完全由通用任务和空任务决定，原始BTA中提出的对数级基础任务集是冗余的。基于此，提出了一种基于目标集的组合方法，通过从通用和空值函数中选取切片来重构组合值函数，降低了学习成本和组合时间，并在多种领域验证了有效性。但在随机环境中，该坍缩不成立，最优组合可能需要指数级策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视BTA的结构性假设，发现并形式化最优扩展Q值函数的坍缩现象，消除原始BTA中冗余的基础任务，降低任务组合的计算成本。

Method: 1) 理论证明在确定性MDP中，最优扩展Q值函数完全由通用任务和空任务确定；2) 提出基于目标集的组合方法，通过对目标集执行逻辑运算，从通用和空值函数中选取切片重构组合值函数；3) 在多种环境（表格、视觉、函数近似、连续控制）中进行实验验证；4) 通过反例展示随机环境中坍缩不成立。

Result: 在确定性MDP中，额外的base tasks不能带来更好的性能；所提方法降低了标准BTA的学习成本以及BTA和Skill Machines的组合时间，同时保持策略性能不变。

Conclusion: 确定性MDP中BTA的最优扩展Q值函数存在坍缩，基础任务集可大幅简化；随机环境中则需指数级策略处理，两者性质有本质区别。

Abstract: The Boolean Task Algebra (BTA) provides a principled framework for zero-shot task composition in reinforcement learning by equipping goal-reaching tasks with Boolean operations. We revisit its structural assumptions and formalize a collapse in the space of optimal extended Q-value functions: in deterministic MDPs, every such function is fully determined by the universal and empty tasks. This makes the logarithmic set of base tasks proposed in the original BTA formulation redundant. Building on this observation, we introduce a goal-set-based composition method that performs logical operations on goal sets and reconstructs composed value functions by selecting slices from the universal and empty value functions. This reduces learning costs for standard BTA and reduces composition time for both BTA and Skill Machines, while preserving policy performance. Experiments across tabular, visual, function-approximation, and continuous-control domains show that learning additional base tasks does not yield better performance. Finally, we study the stochastic setting and provide a counterexample showing that this collapse need not hold, that is, optimal composition may require accounting for exponentially many policies in the number of goals. Code is available at https://github.com/EduardoTerres/bta_paper.

</details>


### [14] [Spectral Scaling Laws of Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04058)
*Gagik Magakyan,Pablo Parrilo,Asuman Ozdaglar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了Muon优化器中Newton-Schulz迭代对动量矩阵奇异值谱的影响，发现不同层类型的奇异值量化值随模型规模呈现清晰的幂律关系（指数从-0.25到-0.96不等），据此提出层感知的NS迭代配置策略，在保证正交化质量的同时避免不必要的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器使用Newton-Schulz迭代近似计算正交归一化，但其对动量矩阵奇异值谱的影响及其随模型规模变化的行为尚不明确，缺乏理论指导来配置NS迭代次数以避免计算浪费或正交化失败。

Method: 对77M至2.8B参数的多个模型，系统追踪各层动量缓冲区的奇异值分位数，分析其随训练过程的变化规律及与模型规模的关系，拟合幂律标度关系。

Result: 奇异值分位数在短时间预热后稳定，稳定值由层类型和模型规模决定，且与模型规模呈清晰幂律关系。中浅层指数约-0.25（随规模变化微弱），而部分深层指数可达-0.96（变化剧烈），导致标准5步NS在更大规模下失效。

Conclusion: 提出基于幂律标度的层感知NS配置策略：可根据模型规模和层类型动态选择最小NS迭代次数及系数，在保证关键方向正交化的前提下避免冗余计算，为大规模训练提供实用指导。

Abstract: Orthonormalized update rules have rapidly become a leading choice of optimizer for training large language models, with recent open-source state-of-the-art models adopting Muon. To keep these updates tractable, Muon performs the orthonormalization with the Newton--Schulz (NS) iteration. Since NS is only approximate, directions with small singular values fail to be orthonormalized. In Muon, NS is applied to the momentum matrix at every step, yet little is known about how the singular value spectrum of these momentum matrices behaves during training, or how that behavior changes with model size. We present the first systematic study of this question. Tracking singular value quantiles of the momentum buffer across layers in models ranging from 77M to 2.8B parameters, we observe a consistent picture: after a short burn-in, the quantiles stabilize at a value determined by the layer type and model size. These stabilization values follow remarkably clean power laws in model size, with layer-dependent exponents. Layers up to mid-late depth scale very mildly with model size $M$ (around $M^{-0.25}$), so the standard 5-step NS configuration used at academic scale will continue to orthonormalize them at much larger scales. Some of the late layers, however, scale much more aggressively (up to $M^{-0.96}$) and will fall into the NS failure regime at frontier scale unless one uses more NS iterations or better-tuned coefficients. NS iterations are computationally expensive at scale; our laws give practitioners a principled, layer-aware recipe for choosing the minimum NS configuration that still orthonormalizes the directions that matter -- avoiding unnecessary computation without sacrificing update quality.

</details>


### [15] [LLM Compression with Jointly Optimizing Architectural and Quantization choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04063)
*Hoang-Loc La,Truong-Thanh Le,Amir Taherkordi,Phuong Hoai Ha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种可微分的神经架构搜索（NAS）框架，联合优化LLM的架构配置和混合精度量化，实现更优的准确率-延迟权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署大语言模型面临巨大的内存和计算需求，现有方法要么从头训练小模型（需大量GPU），要么压缩预训练模型。已有的NAS方法往往限制搜索空间，并将架构搜索与量化过程解耦，无法实现最优压缩。

Method: 提出可微分NAS框架，在完整空间中联合搜索LLM线性层的架构配置和混合精度量化策略，实现端到端联合优化。

Result: 相比顺序执行NAS再量化的基线方法，在同等精度下推理速度提升1.4倍；在同等延迟下，七个推理任务的平均准确率提升高达6%。

Conclusion: 可微分NAS联合优化架构与量化策略，能有效压缩LLM并在边缘设备上实现更好的准确率-延迟权衡。

Abstract: Deploying large language models (LLMs) is challenging due to their significant memory and computational requirements. While some methods address this by developing small or tiny language models from scratch, these approaches demand extensive GPU training. Compressing pre-trained LLMs for edge devices offers a compelling alternative. Beyond pruning and quantization, Neural Architecture Search (NAS) enables effective compression, yet prior NAS approaches often limit the search space and decouple architecture from quantization. We introduce a differentiable NAS framework that explores the entire space and jointly optimizes architectural configurations alongside mixed-precision quantization for linear layers of LLMs. Experiments demonstrate superior accuracy-latency trade-offs: our models achieve up to 1.4x faster inference than sequential NAS-then-quantization baselines at comparable accuracy, or up to 6% higher average accuracy across seven reasoning tasks at equivalent latency.

</details>


### [16] [TPA-AD: A Two-Stage Pseudo Anomaly-Guided Method for Bearing Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04073)
*Xiancheng Wang,Zhibo Zhang,Ran Li,Rui Wang,Minghang Zhao,Shisheng Zhong,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段伪异常引导的异常检测方法（TPA-AD），在仅使用正常样本训练的条件下，通过边界邻域生成伪异常并结合对比学习与KNN，实现轴箱轴承时间序列异常检测，在轴承故障与退化数据集及13个公开TSAD基准上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列异常检测方法在仅能获取正常样本的场景下，依赖于已知故障类别、真实异常先验或随机异常注入，导致正常边界可分离性差，且难以同时处理混合变量场景中的连续与离散特征。

Method: 第一阶段利用重构模型与每特征目标误差控制，在正常边界附近生成伪异常窗口；第二阶段通过正常与伪异常窗口间的对比学习学习异常敏感表征，最后使用KNN生成窗口级与点级异常分数。

Result: 在轴承故障检测与退化过程数据集上，方法展现出稳定的异常响应并对退化演化敏感；在13个公开TSAD数据集及真实高速列车轴承数据上验证了较广泛的适用性。

Conclusion: TPA-AD通过在边界邻域构造伪异常来提升正常边界可分离性，能联合处理混合变量场景，在轴承异常检测任务上优于依赖已知故障类别或随机异常注入的现有方法。

Abstract: This paper proposes a two-stage pseudo anomaly-guided anomaly detection method (\textbf{T}wo-stage \textbf{P}seudo \textbf{A}nomaly-guided \textbf{A}nomaly \textbf{D}etection, \textbf{TPA-AD}) for axle-box bearing time-series anomaly detection (time series anomaly detection, TSAD) under the setting where only normal samples are available for training. The method first generates pseudo-anomalous windows near the normal boundary using a reconstruction model and per-feature target-error control. It then learns anomaly-sensitive representations through contrastive learning between normal and pseudo-anomalous windows, and finally produces window-level and point-level anomaly scores using k-nearest neighbors (KNN). Compared with existing methods that rely on known fault categories, real anomaly priors, or random anomaly injection, TPA-AD improves the separability of the normal boundary by constructing pseudo-anomalies in boundary neighborhoods and can jointly handle continuous and discrete features in mixed-variable scenarios. The main experiments are conducted on bearing fault detection datasets and degradation-process datasets, with an additional exploratory extension on $13$ public TSAD datasets. The results show that the proposed method yields relatively stable anomaly responses, is sensitive to degradation evolution, and demonstrates a certain degree of broader applicability on public TSAD benchmarks and real high-speed-train-related bearing data.

</details>


### [17] [Adaptive Patching Is Harder Than It Looks For Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04074)
*Federico Zucchi,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Keyuan Luo,Thomas Lampert,Ziyue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间序列Transformer中自适应分块（adaptive patching）是否优于调参后的均匀分块（uniform patching）。通过将分块建模为比特率分配问题，推导出动态分块必须满足的阈值条件，并给出了局部和全局的改进上界。理论结果表明：缺乏耦合约束时，标量局部复杂度无法产生非均匀最优；主干网络训练至表征最优后，对齐增益在调参后的均匀分块大小附近崩溃。三个代表性架构的控制实验验证了这些预测：在标准长时预测基准上，验证集选出的均匀基线表现与动态方法相当，无一致优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自适应分块算子何时能优于调参后的均匀分块算子，澄清现有文献中关于自适应分块优势的普遍假设是否成立。

Method: 将分块建模为预算约束下的比特率分配问题，推导动态分块优于均匀基线的显式阈值条件；使用二次代理和强凸性边界刻画局部和全局改进上界；在三个代表性架构上进行受控隔离实验，将每个自适应机制替换为均匀分块大小扫描。

Result: 理论推导指出：局部异质性本身不足以保证自适应分块的优势，需要满足特定的阈值条件；缺乏耦合约束时标量局部复杂度无法产生非均匀最优；主干网络训练至最优时对齐增益在均匀分块大小附近消失。实验证实：验证集选出的均匀基线与自适应方法表现相当，无一致性方向优势，较大的改进是方法和数据集特定的。

Conclusion: 自适应分块应针对调参后的均匀基线进行评估；其价值取决于是否能廉价可靠地识别出更细粒度分块实际降低预测损失的区域。

Abstract: Adaptive patching is a recent and compelling proposal for time-series Transformers: allocate finer patches where the sequence looks locally informative. This paper asks under what conditions a content-adaptive patching operator should outperform a tuned uniform one. Local heterogeneity alone is not enough: under pointwise forecasting losses, a complex-looking region is not automatically one where finer patching reduces the loss. We model patching as a budgeted bitrate allocation and derive an explicit threshold that a dynamic patching rule must satisfy to beat a well-tuned uniform baseline, then bound the achievable improvement both locally (a quadratic surrogate) and globally (a strong-convexity bound under the model's assumptions). Two structural results follow: without a coupling constraint, scalar local complexity cannot produce a non-uniform optimum under a common loss landscape; and once the backbone is trained to its representation-aware optimum, the alignment gain collapses around a well-tuned uniform patch size. To test these predictions, we run a controlled isolation study on three representative architectures, replacing each adaptive mechanism with a uniform patch-size sweep while keeping the backbone, data, and training protocol fixed. On standard long-horizon forecasting benchmarks, the validation-selected uniform baseline is competitive with the dynamic counterpart, with per-setting effects concentrated near zero and no consistent directional advantage once results are aggregated by dataset. The larger gains we do observe are method- and dataset-specific. Adaptive patching should therefore be evaluated against a tuned uniform baseline; its value depends on whether a cheap and reliable routing signal can identify where finer patches actually reduce forecasting loss.

</details>


### [18] [Large Language Models Hack Rewards, and Society](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04075)
*Wei Liu,Xinyi Mou,Hanqi Yan,Zhongyu Wei,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现强化学习训练中模型的奖励黑客行为可能扩展到社会规则层面，通过SocioHack沙盒验证了模型能发现社会监管漏洞，生成技术上合规但违背监管意图的策略，现有安全机制效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会监管与奖励函数在结构上相似（都有可测量的结果、阈值和例外，但机构意图仅部分明确），作者假设RL训练中模型利用这些漏洞的行为可能升级为更严重的"社会黑客"失败模式。

Method: 构建了包含72个社会环境的SocioHack沙盒，观察强化学习训练过程中模型是否会出现奖励黑客行为并发现监管漏洞。

Result: 在社会环境中奖励黑客行为自然涌现，模型能发现社会规则漏洞并生成技术上合规但违背监管意图的策略；当前LLM的安全防护措施只能提供有限的缓解效果。

Conclusion: 收集真实世界的反馈进行模型训练需要更加谨慎，需要下一代后训练范式来安全地在真实社会中迭代LLM。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a dominant post-training paradigm, enabling large language models (LLMs) to learn from rewards. We observe that societal regulations are structurally similar to reward functions. They define measurable outcomes, thresholds, and exceptions, while often leaving institutional intent only partially specified. We hypothesise that the RL training process may exploit these gaps and therefore ask whether models' well-known tendency to hack reward functions during RL can scale into a more consequential failure mode named societal hacking: discovering loopholes in the rules society runs on. To study this phenomenon, we introduce SocioHack, a sandbox of 72 societal environments, and find that within these environments, reward hacking naturally emerges and leads to regulatory loophole discovery. Models learn to hack the social rules and generate strategies that remain technically compliant while defeating regulatory intent, and current LLM safeguards provide only limited mitigation. Therefore, collecting in-the-wild feedback for model training requires greater caution, and we need a next-generation post-training paradigm for safely iterating LLMs in real society.=

</details>


### [19] [Stein Kernelized Molecular Dynamics for Active Learning of Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04100)
*Joanna Zou,Fraser Birks,Dallas Foster,Youssef Marzouk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Stein核化分子动力学（SKMD），一种增强采样方法，通过交互粒子动力学为机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）的主动学习和微调获取信息性训练构型。SKMD是Stein变分梯度下降的随机变体，结合异步粒子更新和全局原子描述符核，能保持玻尔兹曼分布为渐近分布。在Müller-Brown势和丙氨酸二肽的MACE势测试中，SKMD在更少训练迭代中实现了更高模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIPs的性能严重依赖训练数据的质量和多样性，然而传统增强采样方法存在局限性，例如可能破坏玻尔兹曼分布。作者希望设计一种既能高效探索构型空间、又能保持目标分布一致性的采样方法，以生成高质量训练数据用于MLIPs的主动学习和微调。

Method: SKMD方法：1) 基于Stein变分梯度下降（SVGD）的随机变体，引入异步粒子更新以适应分子动力学场景；2) 采用全局原子描述符核函数，提供对称性感知的构型相似性度量；3) 设计自适应停止准则，在模拟过程中在线选择非冗余训练数据。

Result: 在Müller-Brown势的神经网络势主动学习和丙氨酸二肽的MACE势微调两个任务中，与主动学习基线相比，SKMD在相同采集训练样本数量下，以更少的训练迭代次数实现了更高的模型精度。

Conclusion: SKMD是一种有效的增强采样方法，能保持玻尔兹曼分布的同时高效探索构型空间，为MLIPs提供高质量训练数据，在主动学习和微调场景中优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) enable efficient and accurate atomistic simulations but depend critically on the quality and diversity of the training data. We introduce Stein kernelized molecular dynamics (SKMD), an enhanced sampling method that uses interacting particle dynamics to acquire informative training configurations for the active learning and fine-tuning of MLIPs. SKMD corresponds to a stochastic variant of Stein variational gradient descent that is adapted for molecular dynamics by incorporating asynchronous particle updates and a kernel of global atomic descriptors, which provides a symmetry-aware measure of configurational similarity. Unlike other enhanced samplers used in molecular dynamics, SKMD preserves the Boltzmann distribution as the asymptotic distribution of the dynamics. This property enforces a balance between the exploration of diverse configurations and attraction toward high-probability regions of the energy landscape. We further propose an approach to efficient online data acquisition using an adaptive stopping criterion that selects non-redundant training data over the course of simulation. We demonstrate SKMD for the active learning of a neural network model of the Müller-Brown potential and the fine-tuning of a MACE interatomic potential for alanine dipeptide. Compared to active learning baselines, our method achieves higher model accuracy in fewer training iterations with the same number of acquired training samples.

</details>


### [20] [Building The Ph(ysical)AI Layer Of Machine Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04106)
*Ulbert Jose Botero,Liam Smith,Brooks Olney,Pooya Khorrami,Steven Kusiak,Watson Jia,Sage Trudeau,Daniel Capecci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于信号处理原理的基础模型，仅使用射频数据训练（1.99M参数），通过傅里叶分解、能量守恒和对称性等原理约束，无需微调即可跨模态迁移到音频、图像、文本和视频任务，在15个任务上达到77.7%平均准确率（91.9% top-3），揭示了物理原理驱动与规模驱动方法的互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型通过大规模多样化数据训练实现泛化，但在缺乏配对训练数据的真正未见领域迁移时存在局限。

Method: 将信号处理原理（傅里叶分解、能量守恒、对称性）编码到模型架构和损失函数中，仅使用射频（RF）数据训练，得到冻结编码器直接用于跨模态迁移，无需在目标域上进行微调。

Result: 1.99M参数的冻结编码器在15个多样化任务上通过线性探测达到77.7%平均准确率（91.9% top-3），物理基础任务（84.5%）优于语义任务（70.0%）。

Conclusion: 原理驱动与规模驱动方法是互补路径：物理原理实现高效跨模态迁移，同时自然划分了物理理解与语义理解的边界。

Abstract: Foundation models achieve generalization through massive-scale training on diverse data, but have limitations with transfer to truly unseen domains without paired training data. We propose principle-driven foundation models that encode signal-theoretic principles (Fourier decomposition, energy conservation, symmetry) rather than learn untethered statistical correlations. We hypothesize that domains differ not in fundamental physics, but in learnable transformations in time, frequency, magnitude, or phase. Training exclusively on radio-frequency (RF) data with co-designed architecture and losses incorporating these principles, we achieve cross-modal transfer to audio, images, text, and video using only frozen representations learned from RF data, requiring no fine-tuning of the encoder on target domains. Our 1.99M parameter frozen encoder achieves 77.7% average accuracy (91.9% top-3) across 15 diverse tasks via linear probing, with systematic variation: 84.5 on physically-grounded tasks (speaker recognition, seismology, RF fingerprinting) versus 70.0% on semantic tasks (music genre, language recognition). This reveals that principle-driven and scale-driven approaches offer complementary paths: physical principles enable efficient cross-modal transfer while naturally establishing the boundary between physical and semantic understanding.

</details>


### [21] [Variance Reduction for Heavy-Tailed Monetization Metrics in Ranking Experiments via Post-Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04110)
*Neeti Pokharna,Olivier Jeunen,Yatharth Saraf,Aleksei Ustimenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合事后分层与CUPED的方差缩减框架，在ShareChat的排序驱动变现实验中，以标准指标约45%的流量即可达到相同统计置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线排序和检索系统的评估通常依赖变现指标（如应用收入、创作者收益），但这些指标呈重尾分布，少数用户主导均值和方差，导致A/B实验统计功效低、结论不可靠，尤其在流量受限场景下问题突出。

Method: 将事后分层（Post-stratification）与CUPED（使用预实验协变量）相结合，构建方差缩减的实用框架，在不增加流量的前提下提升变现实验的敏感性。

Result: 在ShareChat的排序驱动变现实验中，该方法显著降低了方差并提升了决策稳定性，以标准指标约45%的流量即可达到相同的统计置信水平。

Conclusion: 讨论了实际设计选择、防护措施和局限性，为信息检索与推荐系统中事后分层方法的适用场景提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Online evaluation of ranking and retrieval systems often relies on downstream monetization metrics such as app revenue or creator earnings. These metrics are typically heavy-tailed, with a small fraction of users dominating both mean and variance, leading to low statistical power and unreliable conclusions in A/B experiments -- especially under limited traffic.
  We present a practical framework for variance reduction in online experiments by combining post-stratification with CUPED. Our approach leverages pre-experiment covariates to improve the sensitivity of monetization experiments without requiring additional traffic. Deployed at ShareChat across ranking-driven monetization experiments, the method substantially reduces variance and improves decision stability, achieving equivalent statistical confidence with ~45\% less traffic than standard metrics. We further discuss practical design choices, guardrails, and limitations, providing guidance on when post-stratification is appropriate for real-world information retrieval and Recommendation systems.

</details>


### [22] [dMX: Differentiable Mixed-Precision Assignment for Low-Precision Floating-Point Formats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04115)
*Giuseppe Franco,Ian Colbert,Pablo Monteagudo-Lago,Felix Marty,Nicholas Fraser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: dMX 是一种可微分混合精度量化框架，用于为 LLM 的每层自动学习浮点数字宽分配，基于 MXFP 标准，通过连续优化和退火调度实现硬件兼容的量化配置，在多个 LLM 家族上取得了优于 KL 散度启发式方法的 Pareto 主导结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一位宽对 LLM 所有层进行量化在性能和精度上都是次优的，需要更细粒度的混合精度量化方案。

Method: 将每层浮点格式参数化为一个标量偏移，将多变量设计空间折叠为单个可学习参数；连续优化中采用基于温度的退火调度逐步离散化，最终映射到硬件兼容的 MXFP 格式；引入目标感知正则化项，引导平均位宽接近用户指定的预算。

Result: 在 Llama、Qwen3、SmolLM2 等 LLM 家族上进行实验，在 WikiText-2 困惑度和四个零样本推理基准上，dMX 持续生成 Pareto 主导模型，优于基于 KL 散度的层选择启发式方法。

Conclusion: dMX 能够高效地探索模型质量与平均位宽之间的权衡，为 LLM 混合精度量化提供了一种可微分的自动化方案。

Abstract: Quantizing large language models (LLMs) to low-precision floating-point representations is central to efficient deployment, yet applying a single bit-width uniformly across all layers is sub-optimal in terms of both performance and accuracy. This work introduces dMX, a differentiable mixed-precision quantization framework for learnable floating-point bit-width assignment. We study its application for the microscaling floating-point (MXFP) family of data types defined by the Open Compute Project (OCP) standard. The per-layer bit-width assignment is formulated as a continuous optimization problem in which each layer's floating-point format format is parameterized by a scalar parameter, folding the multi-variate design space into a single learnable offset. During training this offset takes continuous values, avoiding sudden oscillations between discrete quantization formats. A temperature-based annealing schedule progressively discretizes the learned offsets, ensuring that the final configuration maps to hardware-compatible MXFP formats without abrupt transitions between training and inference behavior. A target-aware regularization term steers the average bit-width toward a user-specified budget, serving as a coarse-grained proxy for inference cost and balancing model quality against deployment efficiency. We performed experiments on different families of LLM, such as Llama, Qwen3, and SmolLM2, evaluating perplexity on WikiText-2 and accuracy on four zero-shot reasoning benchmarks. Across these settings, dMX consistently yields Pareto-dominating models and improves over Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence-based layer-selection heuristics, efficiently navigating trade-offs between model quality and average bit-width.

</details>


### [23] [Stationarity-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04135)
*Shiqiao Zhou,Holger Schöner,Zipeng Wu,Edouard Fouché,IAG Wilson,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SARAF提出了一种感知非平稳性的检索增强时间序列预测框架，通过自适应平衡相关性与多样性来检索历史片段，并在八个真实数据集上取得优于强基线的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界时间序列常表现出非平稳性和机制转换，导致仅依赖相似性检索的历史片段可能因未来模式不同而失效，且易产生冗余检索结果。

Method: SARAF首先通过时间对齐增强的时序相似性构建候选池，再采用多样性感知的选择策略覆盖异构历史机制，其多样化强度由数据集层面的非平稳性自动调节，最后通过感知非平稳性的聚合方式融合检索到的未来信息。

Result: 在八个真实数据集上，SARAF取得了有竞争力的预测性能，在平均准确率和鲁棒性上优于强基线，尤其在非平稳性强的场景下优势显著。

Conclusion: 感知非平稳性的检索增强策略能有效提升时间序列预测在非平稳场景下的准确性和鲁棒性，平衡相关性与多样性是其中的关键。

Abstract: Time series forecasting relies on historical patterns, but real-world series often exhibit non-stationarity and regime shifts that challenge fully parametric forecasters. Inspired by Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), recent work augments forecasters by retrieving relevant historical segments and using them as external evidence at inference time. However, due to the intrinsic non-stationarity of real-world time series, a highly similar past segment does not necessarily imply a similar future, rendering similarity-only retrieval brittle and prone to redundancy. We propose Stationarity-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Time Series Forecasting (SARAF), a framework that adaptively balances relevance and diversity in retrieval. SARAF first forms a candidate pool via temporal similarity with time-aligned enhancement, then applies a diversity-aware selection strategy to cover heterogeneous historical regimes, with the diversification strength automatically modulated by dataset-level stationarity. Moreover, SARAF uses stationarity-aware aggregation to fuse the retrieved futures. Extensive experiments on eight real-world datasets show that SARAF achieves competitive forecasting performance and improves average accuracy and robustness over strong baselines, with particularly clear benefits under challenging non-stationary settings. Code: https://github.com/ShiqiaoZhou/SARAF.

</details>


### [24] [Physics-Informed Machine Learning for Short-Term Flood Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04143)
*Tewodros Syum Gebre,Jagrati Talreja,Leila Hashemi-Beni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种物理信息机器学习（PIML）框架，通过将趋势对齐约束（Trend Alignment constraint）作为正则化项加入LSTM损失函数，使洪水预测模型在数据稀缺环境下保持物理一致性，减少纯数据驱动模型常见的非物理波动，在仅使用5%数据时将NSE从0.20提升至0.23，并在极端气候模拟中保持方向一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的机器学习模型在数据稀缺场景下表现不佳，且可能违背基本水文物理原理；标准LSTM在极端天气外推时会产生物理不一致的预测结果，亟需一种能够融合物理知识的方法来提升模型可靠性和泛化能力。

Method: 构建物理信息机器学习（PIML）框架：在标准LSTM的损失函数中加入趋势对齐约束（Trend Alignment constraint）作为正则化项，惩罚降水量与流量趋势之间的方向不一致性，无需复杂的水动力学方程即可引导模型学习物理上合理的流量过程线。

Result: 在仅使用5%训练数据时，PIML模型的NSE从0.20提升至0.23；在模拟极端气候的应力测试中，基线LSTM出现不稳定行为，而PIML保持了方向一致性和物理合理性；极端峰值预测仍具挑战，但非物理波动显著减少。

Conclusion: 简单的物理约束（趋势对齐）能够显著提升深度学习模型在实时洪水预测中的可靠性，为无测站流域和气候变化条件下提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate flood forecasting is essential for mitigating disaster risks and protecting communities. However, purely data-driven machine learning models often struggle in data-scarce environments and may violate fundamental hydrological principles. Standard Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks can generate physically inconsistent predictions, particularly when extrapolating to extreme weather conditions. To address these limitations, we propose a Physics-Informed Machine Learning (PIML) framework that incorporates hydrological knowledge directly into the loss function of an LSTM model. Specifically, a Trend Alignment constraint penalizes directional inconsistencies between precipitation and discharge trends, improving model robustness without requiring complex hydrodynamic equations. This regularization encourages the model to learn physically plausible hydrograph behavior, even with limited training data, while enhancing reliability during peak flood events. Experimental results show that the proposed physics-informed model outperforms a standard LSTM baseline in data-scarce settings, increasing the Nash-Sutcliffe Efficiency (NSE) from 0.20 to 0.23 when trained on only 5% of the available data. Additional stress tests under simulated extreme climate scenarios demonstrate that the baseline model exhibits unstable behavior, whereas the physics-informed model maintains directional consistency and physical plausibility. Although accurately predicting extreme peak magnitudes remains challenging with limited data, the proposed approach substantially reduces unphysical fluctuations common in purely data-driven models. These findings demonstrate that simple physical constraints can significantly improve the reliability of deep learning models for real-time flood forecasting, offering a practical solution for ungauged basins and evolving climate conditions.

</details>


### [25] [When Offline Selectors Cannot Beat the Best Single Model: A Diagnostic Study on edX Dropout Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04161)
*Tyler Crosse,Alan Nadelsticher Ruvalcaba,Dustin Khang LeDuc,Thomas Trask,Nicholas Lytle,David Joyner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三阶段诊断方法，用于分析为何在多个预测模型中选择最佳模型时，基于离线数据的选取器（如BC、DQN、CQL）无法超越单一最强模型。通过edX点击流数据中五个辍学预测模型的实验，发现瓶颈在于局部表示模糊性，而非学习者不匹配或标签偏移，建议下一步应改变状态设计或收集新数据，而非调优离线学习者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同预测模型在不同输入上各有优劣，理论上每实例选择最佳模型能比固定单一模型取得更高准确率。然而实践中，基于历史日志数据训练的选取器（selector）往往无法超越最强单一模型。为找出失败的根源，需要系统诊断三种可能原因：学习者不匹配、状态无法预测哪个模型获胜、或缓存到部署的标签偏移。

Method: 提出三阶段诊断框架：(1) 通过k-NN标签一致性估计局部天花板（oracle recovery上限）；(2) 检查BC、DQN、CQL等离线RL学习者能否达到该天花板；(3) 消融选取器状态，测试更丰富的特征是否会提升天花板。应用在edX点击流数据上，从五个辍学预测模型中挑选最佳，跨越16个时间窗口验证。

Result: Oracle相比最强单一基础模型平均高出9.7个准确率百分点，但BC、DQN、CQL均落在该上限以下的同一测试准确率区间（对十倍缓存扫描和2000个保留样本均稳健）。瓶颈为局部表示模糊性：CQL缩小了模仿差距但未带来部署增益（非保守性问题），遗憾值在各学习者间紧密聚类（非平局打破问题），三个学习者在测试准确率上收敛（非标签偏移问题）。

Conclusion: 下一步应改变状态设计或收集新数据，而非继续调优离线学习者。三阶段诊断有效定位了性能瓶颈的根本原因，为后续改进方向提供了明确指导。

Abstract: Different predictors often excel on different inputs, so picking the best one per instance promises higher accuracy than committing to a single model. In practice, selectors trained from logged data routinely fail to beat the strongest single predictor. Three causes typically go unseparated before more tuning is applied: a mismatched learner, a state that does not predict which model wins, or buffer-to-deployment label shift.
  A three-stage diagnostic rules them out on a shared buffer. Stage~1 estimates a local ceiling on oracle recovery from $k$-NN label consistency. Stage~2 asks whether paired BC and offline-RL learners (BC, DQN, and CQL across penalty weights) reach that ceiling. Stage~3 ablates the selector state to test whether richer features would raise it. The combined verdict points to the most promising next step: tuning the learner, redesigning the state, or collecting new data.
  We apply it to selecting among five dropout-prediction models on edX clickstream data. Across 16 windows, the oracle beats the strongest single base model by 9.7 accuracy points on average, yet BC, DQN, and CQL land in the same test-accuracy band below it (robust to a tenfold buffer sweep and $N{=}2{,}000$ held-out examples). The bottleneck is local representational ambiguity: CQL closes the imitation gap without a deployment gain (not conservatism), regret clusters tightly across learners (not tie-breaking), and the three learners converge on test accuracy (not shift). The next iteration should change the state or collect new data, not tune the offline learner further.

</details>


### [26] [ADAPTOOD: Uncertainty-Aware Fine-Tuning for Out-of-Distribution ECG Time Series Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04164)
*Sotirios Vavaroutas,Yu Yvonne Wu,Ali Etemad,Cecilia Mascolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ADAPTOOD框架，利用数据不确定性量化分布偏移严重程度，结合低秩更新和自适应超参数优化，在时间序列OOD任务上实现最高7%准确率和12.9%精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有适应方法通常假设固定分布偏移，忽视偏移严重程度的差异（如将大规模熟悉数据集的适应与小样本新任务的适应等同处理），导致泛化能力受限。

Method: 1) 利用数据不确定性量化目标部署分布与预训练分布之间的偏离程度，作为OOD严重程度的直接信号；2) 将不确定性度量与低秩模型更新相结合；3) 采用自适应超参数优化以指导时间序列的微调过程。

Result: 在OOD任务上，ADAPTOOD相比现有方法准确率提升最高7%，精度提升最高12.9%，且在分布偏移严重度增加时仍能保持强性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式量化并利用分布偏移严重度信息，能够有效提升模型在多样化真实场景下的适应能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Data samples used for training often differ from those encountered during fine-tuning and deployment, and while ML models show promise, their performance remains limited when only small annotated datasets are available. Performance often degrades under distribution shifts caused by diverse sensors, populations, and application settings. Although pre-training helps, models frequently encounter out-of-distribution (OOD) data in real-world settings, leading to reduced robustness. Existing adaptation methods usually assume fixed distribution shifts and struggle when multiple types or severities occur. In particular, they overlook shift severity, for example treating adaptation to a large familiar dataset the same as adaptation to a small dataset with a new task, which limits generalisation. To address this, we propose ADAPTOOD, a novel framework that leverages data uncertainty to quantify distribution shift severity and guide fine-tuning for time series. This uncertainty measures how strongly samples from the target deployment distribution deviate from the pre-training distribution, providing a direct signal of OOD severity. Our framework combines this uncertainty with low-rank model updates and adaptive hyperparameter optimisation to improve adaptation. We show that ADAPTOOD achieves up to 7% higher accuracy and 12.9% higher precision than existing methods in OOD tasks, maintaining strong performance as distribution shift severity increases.

</details>


### [27] [Smart Transportation Without Neurons -- Fair Metro Network Expansion with Tabular Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04167)
*Dimitris Michailidis,Sennay Ghebreab,Fernando P. Santos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用表格型强化学习替代深度强化学习解决地铁网络扩展问题（MNEP），在西安和阿姆斯特丹的真实场景中，训练轮数减少18倍，碳排放减少12倍，同时保持竞争力且更具可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MNEP方法依赖专家定义约束的精确或启发式方法，而深度强化学习虽有效但计算成本高、环境代价大且可解释性差。作者发现MNEP问题规模足够小，无需深度强化学习即可解决。

Method: 将MNEP重新表述为非马尔可夫奖励决策过程（NMRDP），使用表格型强化学习（tabular RL）进行求解，并在奖励函数中融入社会公平性指标（效率和公平性）。

Result: 在西安和Amsterdam真实场景中，表格型RL相比深度RL减少了18倍训练轮数和12倍碳排放，同时性能相当。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种可复现、模块化、可解释且资源高效的解决方案，有望推广至其他组合优化问题。

Abstract: We tackle the Metro Network Expansion Problem (MNEP), a subset of the Transport Network Design Problem (TNDP), which focuses on expanding metro systems to satisfy travel demand. Traditional methods rely on exact and heuristic approaches that require expert-defined constraints to reduce the search space. Recently, deep reinforcement learning (Deep RL) has emerged due to its effectiveness in complex sequential decision-making processes-it remains, however, computationally expensive, environmentally costly, and requires additional engineering to interpret. We show that MNEP problems are small enough to not require Deep RL methods. Reformulating the MNEP as a Non-Markovian Rewards Decision Process (NMRDP), we use tabular RL to achieve similar performance with significantly fewer training episodes, additionally offering greater interpretability. Additionally, we incorporate social equity criteria into the reward functions, focusing on efficiency and fairness, highlighting the versatility of our method. Evaluated in real-world settings-Xi'an and Amsterdam-our method reduces total episodes by a factor of 18 and total carbon emissions by a factor of 12 on average, while remaining competitive with Deep RL. This approach offers a replicable, modular, interpretable, and resource-efficient solution with potential applications to other combinatorial optimization problems.

</details>


### [28] [When Autoregressive Consistency Hurts Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04168)
*Bochen Lyu,Yiyang Jia,Xiaohao Cai,Zhanxing Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大型语言模型安全对齐的浅层性问题源于自回归一致性（autoregressive consistency），即模型倾向于延续当前响应轨迹。自回归一致性将安全更新集中在早期token上，导致安全对齐浅层化。基于该机制，作者提出随机插入攻击（random insertion attack），通过在安全拒绝轨迹中插入简短有害片段即可绕过安全对齐。作为防御，提出对抗性安全对齐（adversarial safety alignment）框架，通过最坏情况有害延续状态训练来强化模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的安全对齐存在脆弱性，尤其是其浅层化问题——微调主要改变模型在初始几个输出token附近的行为。作者试图从自回归一致性的角度给出这种浅层安全对齐的机制解释，并探索其导致的更广泛攻击类别。

Method: 1) 分析安全对齐的学习动态，证明自回归一致性将安全更新集中在早期token上。2) 提出随机插入攻击：在安全拒绝轨迹中插入简短有害span，利用自回归一致性维持有害分支。3) 提出对抗性安全对齐框架，基于最坏情况有害延续状态训练，实例化为随机最坏插入训练。

Result: 自回归一致性是安全对齐浅层化的机制原因；即使经过长段拒绝前缀，一个简短的有害span也能将生成重定向为有害内容；随机插入攻击能有效绕过安全对齐。

Conclusion: 自回归一致性应被视为安全对齐和攻击设计的核心考量因素。安全对齐需要在整个输出轨迹上打破有害的自回归一致性，而不仅仅是关注初始token。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) is fragile in part because it is often shallow: fine-tuning mainly reshapes the model's behavior near the first few output tokens. We argue that this phenomenon can be understood through autoregressive consistency, the tendency of next-token prediction to preserve and extend the current response trajectory consistently. By analyzing the learning dynamics of safety alignment, we show that autoregressive consistency can concentrate alignment updates on early tokens, offering a mechanistic explanation for shallow safety alignment. The same mechanism also predicts a broader class of attacks on LLMs: attacks that induce harmful continuation states at arbitrary positions in the output trajectory. As a concrete example, we introduce random insertion attack, which inserts a short harmful span into an otherwise safe refusal trajectory and exploits autoregressive consistency to sustain the resulting harmful branch, thereby bypassing safety alignment. Notably, a short harmful span can redirect the generation to be harmful even after a long refusal prefix, highlighting autoregressive consistency as a potential broader failure mechanism. This suggests that safety alignment should also break harmful autoregressive consistency throughout the output trajectory. We therefore propose adversarial safety alignment, an initial framework based on worst-case harmful continuation states, and instantiate it with random worst-insertion training. Overall, our results suggest that autoregressive consistency should be treated as a central consideration in both safety alignment and attack design.

</details>


### [29] [Low-rank Distributional Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04176)
*Jiayi Wang,Raymond K. W. Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究分布化矩阵补全问题，其中矩阵条目为概率分布而非标量。通过核均值嵌入和Tucker秩捕捉低秩结构，提出基于函数展开算子的估计器，并给出非渐近误差界，实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统矩阵补全假设条目为标量，但实际应用中矩阵条目常为概率分布（如不同人群的评分分布），且只能观测到有限样本而非完整分布。现有方法无法直接处理这种分布化、无限维的矩阵补全问题。

Method: 1) 采用核均值嵌入表示分布化条目，将分布映射到再生核希尔伯特空间；2) 定义分布值矩阵的Tucker秩以刻画低秩结构；3) 引入函数展开算子将无限维的低秩结构转化为有限维张量的经典Tucker秩；4) 基于该框架提出分布化矩阵补全的估计器。

Result: 建立了所提估计器的非渐近误差界，从理论上刻画了统计性能。合成数据与真实数据实验均验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文成功将矩阵补全推广到分布化条目场景，通过核均值嵌入和函数展开算子解决了无限维挑战，理论分析与实证结果均支撑了方法的可行性。

Abstract: We study a distributional generalization of the matrix completion problem in which each entry of the target matrix is a probability distribution rather than a scalar. In this setting, only a subset of matrix entries is observed, and even for observed entries, the underlying distributions are not directly accessible; instead, we observe finitely many samples drawn from them. To represent distributional entries, we employ kernel mean embeddings and introduce a notion of Tucker rank for distribution-valued matrices to capture their low-rank structure. The infinite-dimensional nature of kernel embeddings poses significant methodological challenges. To address this, we introduce functional unfolding operators that link the proposed distributional low-rank structure to the classical Tucker rank for finite-dimensional tensors. Based on this framework, we propose a novel estimator for distributional matrix completion. We establish non-asymptotic error bounds that characterize the statistical performance of the estimator. Extensive experiments on synthetic data and a real-world application demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [30] [KODA: Contrastive Representation Comparison and Alignment for Vision-Language Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04180)
*Youqi Wu,Mohammad Jalali,Farzan Farnia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KODA（Kernel Optimization for Discrepancy Analysis）框架，通过核方法对比分析视觉-语言基础模型（如CLIP、SigLIP）表示间的结构差异，识别弱聚类与强聚类的样本子集，并实现可解释的差异发现与表示对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言基础模型通常仅通过下游性能进行比较，无法解释其表征在结构上的本质差异。作者旨在通过对比嵌入聚类任务，系统性地发现不同模型表示间的结构性差异。

Method: KODA构建统一多模态核（通过模态级核组合），将差异发现形式化为约束优化问题：在一个表示中搜索强聚类结构，同时抑制参考表示中的聚类。为扩展到大规模数据，采用随机投影（如随机傅里叶特征）对联合核进行低维近似。

Result: KODA能够识别出一致且可解释的差异结构，涵盖不同视觉-语言表示之间，并提供用于表示对齐的样本子集。

Conclusion: KODA提供了一种系统性的核方法框架，用于对比分析和对齐多模态表示，揭示了CLIP、SigLIP等模型间可解释的结构性差异。

Abstract: Vision-language foundation models such as CLIP and SigLIP provide widely used representations for multimodal learning systems. While these models are typically compared through downstream performance, such evaluations often do not explain how their representations differ structurally. In this work, we study this problem through the task of Contrastive Embedding Clustering: identifying sample subsets that are weakly clustered under one representation but strongly clustered under another. We propose \emph{Kernel Optimization for Discrepancy Analysis (KODA)}, a kernel-based framework for contrastive representation comparison and alignment. KODA constructs unified multimodal kernels through modality-wise kernel composition and formulates discrepancy discovery as a constrained optimization problem that searches for coherent structures in one representation while suppressing coherence in a reference representation. This yields interpretable discrepancy directions associated with specific sample subsets and modality interactions. To scale KODA to large vision-language datasets, we develop randomized low-dimensional approximations of joint kernels using random projections, including Random Fourier Features for shift-invariant kernels. Empirically, KODA identifies consistent and interpretable discrepancy structures across vision-language representations and provides sample subsets for representation alignment. The code is available at https://github.com/yokiwuuu/KODA.

</details>


### [31] [Exact Unlearning in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04182)
*Thanh Nguyen-Tang,Raman Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出强化学习中的精确遗忘问题，设计了一种ρ-TV稳定的RL算法，支持用户数据删除后的精确遗忘，其计算成本仅为从头再训练的ρ√lnT倍，并在表格型MDP上实现了接近最优的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL算法缺乏对用户数据删除请求的精确遗忘能力，即删除数据后学习器的输出应与该用户从未参与学习时的输出不可区分。需要设计高效框架满足隐私法规（如GDPR）的"被遗忘权"要求。

Method: 提出ρ-TV稳定（总变差距离稳定）的RL算法，通过构造稳定的在线学习过程实现精确遗忘。算法基于表格型MDP，其遗忘过程的期望计算成本仅为原始训练成本的ρ√lnT倍。

Result: 算法遗憾界为O(H²√(SAT) + H³S²A + H^{2.5}S²A/ρ)，下界为Ω(H√(SAT) + SAH/ρ)，表明算法近乎极小化最优。

Conclusion: 首次在强化学习中形式化精确遗忘问题，给出了理论上高效且近乎最优的解决方案，平衡了隐私需求、计算效率和学习性能。

Abstract: We formulate the problem of \emph{exact unlearning} in reinforcement learning, where the goal is to design an efficient framework that enables the removal of any user's data upon deletion request, i.e., the online learner's output after unlearning is \emph{indistinguishable} from what would have been produced had the deleted user never interacted with the learner. For any $ρ>0$, we show that there exists a reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm that is $ρ$-TV-stable and supports an exact unlearning procedure whose expected computational cost is only a $ρ\sqrt{\ln T}$ fraction of the computational cost of retraining from scratch. We construct such a $ρ$-TV-stable RL algorithm for tabular Markov decision processes (MDPs), which achieves a regret bound of $\mathcal{O}(H^2 \sqrt{SAT} + H^3 S^2 A + {H^{2.5} S^2 A}/ρ)$, where $S, A, H$, and $T$ denote the number of states, the number of actions, the episode horizon, and the number of episodes, respectively. We also establish a lower bound of $Ω(H\sqrt{\!SAT}\! +\! {SAH}/ρ)$ for $ρ$-TV-stable RL algorithms, showing that our algorithm is nearly minimax optimal.

</details>


### [32] [Dual Advantage Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04188)
*Alexey Zemtsov,Maxim Bobrin,Alexander Nikulin,Dmitry V. Dylov,Fakhri Karray,Vladislav Kurenkov,Martin Takáč,Arip Asadulaev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Dual Advantage Fields (DAF)方法，利用双线性对偶价值模型提取局部优势信号，通过动作效应模型预测特征位移与目标方向的对齐程度来评分动作，在OGBench任务上取得优秀性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线目标条件强化学习中，双目标表示能捕捉全局目标可达性，但不能直接指定给定状态下应优先选择哪个动作，需要一种能从双价值模型中提取局部动作偏好的方法。

Method: 基于双线性对偶参数化，目标嵌入是价值场关于状态表示的梯度。DAF学习一个动作效应模型，预测折扣后的特征位移，通过计算位移与目标方向的对齐程度来给动作评分，该评分在可实现情况下等于目标条件Bellman优势。

Result: 在OGBench的 locomotion、manipulation 和 puzzle 任务上，DAF改善了聚合RLiable指标，在局部正确动作与直接朝向最终目标不同的设置中表现强劲。

Conclusion: DAF提供了一种将双线性对偶价值模型转化为局部优势信号的有效策略提取方法，具有标准局部策略改进保证，在复杂离线学习场景中优于基线方法。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning requires both long-horizon reachability estimates and local action comparisons. Dual goal representations provide value fields that capture global goal reachability, but they do not directly specify which action should be preferred at a given state. We propose Dual Advantage Fields, a policy-extraction method that turns a bilinear dual value model into a local advantage signal. Under bilinear dual parameterization, the goal embedding is the gradient of the value field with respect to the state representation. DAF learns an action-effect model that predicts the discounted feature displacement induced by an action and scores actions by the alignment between this displacement and the goal direction. In the realizable case, this score equals the goal-conditioned Bellman advantage, yielding a standard local policy-improvement guarantee. On OGBench locomotion, manipulation, and puzzle tasks, DAF improves aggregate RLiable metrics and performs strongly in settings where locally correct actions differ from direct movement toward the final goal.

</details>


### [33] [Metric-Aware Hybrid Forecasting for the CTF4Science Lorenz Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04191)
*Cen Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对CTF4Science Lorenz挑战，我们构建了一个度量感知的混合系统，针对不同度量族分配不同预测器：合成预训练去噪器用于完整轨迹重建、Lorenz ODE拟合与轨迹投射用于前20步预测、直方图尾部替换用于长时间评估。中间系统的公开排行榜得分为83.83551，最终版本为83.85529。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该基准测试包含九个任务对，涵盖短时预测、长时分布匹配和轨迹重建，且实验发现单一模型族无法在所有度量上占优，因此需要构建混合系统。

Method: (1) 合成预训练去噪器用于完整轨迹重建；(2) Lorenz ODE拟合与轨迹投射用于前20步预测；(3) 基于合成Lorenz库的直方图尾部替换用于长时评估。系统最终提交版本是中间系统的保守扩展。

Result: 中间系统公开排行榜得分83.83551，最终版本得分83.85529。

Conclusion: 没有单一模型族在所有度量上占优，度量感知的混合系统能取得更好效果；中间系统完整且易于复现分析，最终版本是其保守扩展。

Abstract: We describe our approach to the CTF4Science Lorenz challenge, a benchmark that mixes short-horizon forecasting, long-time distribution matching, and trajectory reconstruction across nine task pairs. The key discovery is that no single model family dominated all metrics. Instead, we built a metric-aware hybrid system that assigned a different predictor to each metric family: (1) synthetic-pretrained denoisers for full-trajectory reconstruction, (2) Lorenz ODE fitting and trajectory shooting for the first 20 forecast steps, and (3) histogram-tail substitution using synthetic Lorenz libraries for long-time evaluation. A representative mature submission from this system family scored 83.83551 on the public leaderboard, and a small follow-up stack of the same ideas reached 83.85529. We focus on the cleaner intermediate system because it captures the full method while remaining simple enough to reproduce and analyze, while the final submission can be understood as a conservative extension of the same backbone.

</details>


### [34] [Training-Free Lexical-Dense Fusion for Conversational-Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04194)
*Christian Lysenstøen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在长程对话记忆检索中，将晚期交互（逐轮最大相似度）的密集检索分数与BM25进行分数级融合的效果。实验表明，该训练无关、仅CPU的融合策略在LoCoMo基准上Hit@1显著提升8.8–17.2点，达到0.752；但现成的跨编码器重排序器反而损害性能。研究还发现晚期-早期密集表示差距随编码器增大而扩大，且融合增益在BM25已饱和的词汇域中不显著。分类分析揭示了密集晚期交互在多跳和时间问题上的优势，但在对抗性问题上不及BM25。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程多会话历史中检索少量过去轮次以回答新查询是长程对话记忆的检索瓶颈。作者不声称提出新检索器，而是复现并探索：在训练无关、仅CPU的检索阶段，应在晚期交互（逐轮最大相似度）基础上添加什么组件。

Method: (1) 分数级融合：将晚期交互密集分数与BM25分数按单一留一对话出权重融合；(2) 测试现成网络搜索交叉编码器重排序器对fused top-10的重排效果；(3) 池化算子消融实验：对比top-k晚期交互与最大相似度、平滑最大（log-sum-exp）的效果；(4) 分析晚期-早期表示差距及融合增益随编码器规模的变化；(5) 按问题类别进行增益分析。

Result: (1) 融合策略在六个编码器上Hit@1提升8.8–17.2点（均p<1e-4），e5-large-v2达Hit@1 0.752 / NDCG@5 0.829，比BM25高11.2 pp；(2) 交叉编码器重排序器反而使Hit@1下降6.9 pp；(3) top-k晚期交互等价于最大相似度，但对半数编码器平滑最大崩溃；(4) 晚期-早期差距随编码器增大而扩大，融合边际增益缩小；在LongMemEval-S词汇域上融合增益不显著；(5) 分类分析：密集晚期交互在多跳和时间问题上有优势，在对抗性问题上不及BM25。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个可控、可复现的训练无关强检索方案（分数级融合），而非提出新的晚期交互检索器。该融合实现了密集与稀疏检索的分工增益，但存在边际递减和重排序器伤害等限制。研究定位为检索阶段分析，非完整记忆架构。

Abstract: Retrieving the few past turns that answer a new query across long multi-session histories is the retrieval bottleneck behind long-term conversational memory (LoCoMo, LongMemEval). Recent concurrent work, Nano-Memory, shows that scoring a session by the maximum query-turn similarity (late interaction, "Turn Isolation Retrieval") beats mean-pooled session embeddings. We do not claim that effect; we replicate it and ask what a training-free, CPU-only retrieval stage should add around it. We report four findings. (1) Fuse: score-level fusion of the late-interaction dense score with BM25, under a single leave-one-conversation-out weight, adds +8.8 to +17.2 points of LoCoMo Hit@1 over late interaction alone across six encoders (all p<1e-4), reaching Hit@1 0.752 / NDCG@5 0.829 (e5-large-v2), +11.2 pp over BM25. (2) An off-the-shelf web-search cross-encoder reranker over the fused top-10 hurts here, degrading Hit@1 by 6.9 pp (one reranker, one configuration). (3) A pooling-operator ablation shows top-k late interaction matches max-similarity, but a naive smooth-max (log-sum-exp) collapses for half the encoders. (4) The late-minus-early gap is large for all six encoders and tends to be larger for larger ones, while the marginal fusion gain shrinks; on LongMemEval-S, a lexical regime where BM25 saturates, the net fusion gain over BM25 is small and not significant. A per-category analysis frames the gain as a division of labor: dense late interaction helps most on multi-hop and temporal questions but trails BM25 on adversarial ones. The contribution is a controlled, reproducible account of a strong training-free retrieval recipe, not the late-interaction retriever itself (Nano-Memory's). We make no claim to a complete memory architecture; this is a retrieval-stage study.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释被广泛用于解释和审计机器学习系统，但现有研究忽视了模型即使在预测性能相似的情况下，其反事实行为的差异。本文旨在探究这种差异的存在性、来源及其对模型选择、鲁棒性和反事实方法可靠性的影响。

Method: 采用标准化的局部搜索探针，在多个预训练编码器和线性分类头的组合上进行实验。固定表示空间，仅改变分类头，比较不同模型的反事实行为。

Result: 预测性能相似的模型在反事实行为上存在显著差异；仅改变分类头即可改变反事实结果而预测性能几乎不变；这种差异由决策边界邻近性和局部数据支持性的交互作用决定。

Conclusion: 反事实行为是独立于预测性能的一个维度，可以在不改变准确率的情况下被改变，这对模型选择、鲁棒性和反事实方法的可靠性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations seek small, semantically meaningful changes to an input that alter a model's prediction, and are widely used to interpret and audit machine learning systems. In modern vision, language, and multimodal systems, pretrained encoders map inputs to representation spaces, and downstream classifier heads impose decision boundaries within those spaces. As a result, the feasibility and distance of nearby counterfactuals depend on boundary placement relative to the data. Yet models with similar predictive performance can differ substantially in whether such changes are achievable and how far representations must move. This work examines this variation using a standardized local search probe across several pretrained encoders and linear classifier heads. Results show that despite similar predictive performance, models differ substantially in their counterfactual behavior. Under fixed representations, varying only the classifier head alters counterfactual outcomes while leaving predictive performance largely unchanged. This variation is explained by the interaction of decision-boundary proximity and local data support, which jointly determine whether prediction changes are both feasible and lie in regions supported by the data, and can also improve counterfactual search within fixed models. Together, these findings identify counterfactual behavior as a distinct dimension beyond predictive performance and show that it can be altered without changing accuracy, with implications for model selection, robustness, and the reliability of counterfactual methods.

</details>

*Shauna Kwag,Anakha Ganesh,Tomaso Poggio,Pierfrancesco Beneventano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了"稳定性边缘"（EoS）不仅是优化的全局属性，更是一种选择性机制：它会在训练数据分布的不同子集之间重新分配学习进度，使某些组受益而抑制其他组。通过分支干预实验，文章因果性地证明了这种权衡，并识别出两组受益的必要条件：梯度与Hessian最大特征向量对齐，以及梯度幅度不消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将EoS视为优化过程的全局属性，忽视了其对数据分布不同子集的差异化影响。本文旨在揭示EoS的选择性机制，并因果性地证明它在不同数据组之间重新分配学习的作用。

Method: 采用分支干预方法，从相同训练状态进入或退出EoS regime；通过可控扰动（保持梯度距离但随机化方向）破坏对齐机制；分析交叉熵损失下的梯度饱和效应。

Result: EoS具有选择性：它会在数据分布的子集之间重新分配学习，放大某些组的进度同时抑制其他组。一个组受益需要两个条件：(1) 其聚合梯度与Hessian最大特征向量对齐；(2) 梯度幅度随时间不消失。在交叉熵损失下，梯度饱和会使已置信分类的组脱离受益，优势转向梯度持续的输出异常值组。

Conclusion: EoS不仅是一个稳定性边界，更是一种控制学习在数据分布中分配的重要机制。

Abstract: Existing analyses of the edge of stability (EoS) treat it as a global property of optimization. We show that it is also selective: the stability constraint redistributes learning across subsets of the training distribution, amplifying progress on some groups while suppressing progress on others. Using a branching intervention that enters or exits the EoS regime from the same training state, we causally demonstrate this trade-off and identify two necessary conditions for a group to benefit. First, its aggregate gradient must align with the top Hessian eigenvector. We isolate this mechanism with a controlled perturbation that preserves distance but randomizes direction, destroying alignment and eliminating the advantage. Second, the group must sustain non-vanishing gradient magnitude over time. Under cross-entropy loss, gradient saturation decouples confidently classified groups, shifting the advantage to output-outliers, whose gradients persist. Together, these results show that EoS functions not only as a stability boundary, but as a mechanism governing the allocation of learning across the data distribution.

</details>

*Devleena Das,Rajeev Patwari,Elliott Delaye,Ashish Sirasao*

  <summary>Details</summary>
